# Test source

```ts
  4   | import { QuotePage } from "../../pages/Quote/QuotePage";
  5   | import { QuoteNavBar } from "../../pages/Quote/QuoteNavBar";
  6   | import { ORM } from "../../pages/ORM";
  7   | import { epic, feature, story, step } from "allure-js-commons";
  8   | 
  9   | test.describe("ORM Integration", () => {
  10  |   let navBarPage: NavBarPage;
  11  |   let subNavBarPage: SubNavBarPage;
  12  |   let ormMsgPage: ORM;
  13  |   let quotePage: QuotePage;
  14  |   let quoteNavBar: QuoteNavBar;
  15  | 
  16  |   // Variables
  17  |   let generatedQuoteNumber: string;
  18  |   // let generatedQuoteNumber: string = "10468";
  19  | 
  20  |   let changedPartValues: number[] = [];
  21  |   let changedGrandTotalExTax: number = 0;
  22  |   let changedTaxAmount: number = 0;
  23  |   let changedGrandTotalIncTax: number = 0;
  24  | 
  25  |   test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
  26  |     await epic("ORM Integration");
  27  | 
  28  |     navBarPage = new NavBarPage(page);
  29  |     subNavBarPage = new SubNavBarPage(page);
  30  |     ormMsgPage = new ORM(page);
  31  |     quotePage = new QuotePage(page);
  32  |     quoteNavBar = new QuoteNavBar(page);
  33  | 
  34  |     await page.goto("v2/");
  35  |     await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/v2\/$/);
  36  |   });
  37  | 
  38  |   test("Load RFQ", async ({ page }) => {
  39  |     await navBarPage.openQuoteDropdown();
  40  |     await navBarPage.selectORMMessages();
  41  |     await subNavBarPage.clickNewButton();
  42  | 
  43  |     const { insuranceClaimNo, registrationNo } = await ormMsgPage.pasteRfqXml();
  44  |     let editorValue: string = "";
  45  |     await step("Read current XML content from CodeMirror editor", async () => {
  46  |       editorValue = await page.evaluate(() => {
  47  |         const cmInstance = (document.querySelector(".CodeMirror") as any)
  48  |           .CodeMirror;
  49  |         return cmInstance.getValue();
  50  |       });
  51  |     });
  52  |     await step(
  53  |       `Verify XML editor contains InsuranceClaimNo: ${insuranceClaimNo}`,
  54  |       async () => {
  55  |         expect(editorValue).toContain(
  56  |           `<InsuranceClaimNo>${insuranceClaimNo}</InsuranceClaimNo>`,
  57  |         );
```
